The synthesized subgraph is **ephemeral** — never serialized, rebuilt from the *current* model on every recompile (and `SimulatorView`'s `useEffect([model])` recompiles on every model change). Only the small `Mapping.linked*` config persists. Two layers keep it sound: the ModelContext cascade (layer 1) + the transform's live resolve/guard/clamp (layer 2). The transform resolves the attribute live by id, branches on its live `type`, and (tag) clamps the palette to the live `tagOptions`, so a stale config can never emit a dangling read. Attribute **rename** needs no cascade (synthesis is id-based, never name-based).

### Schema ([types.ts](src/model/types.ts), all optional → old files load unchanged)
- `Mapping.linked?`, `linkedAttributeId?`, `linkedMin?`, `linkedMax?`, `linkedColors?: LinkedColorSet`.
- `ColorStop { position: number; r,g,b }` — gradient stop; `position` is in **[0,1]** (same space as the Color Scale node) and is mapped onto `[linkedMin, linkedMax]` at compile time (raw attribute value fed as `t`; ColorScale clamps outside the range).
- `LinkedColorSet { gradient?: ColorStop[]; method?: string; tag?: RGB[] }`. `gradient` covers bool (2 stops at 0/1) / float / integer; `method` is the interpolation curve; `tag` is per-option colors. Absent sub-fields → auto defaults generated by the transform.

### `categoricalColor` node ([CategoricalColorNode.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/CategoricalColorNode.ts)) — the only NEW emitter
First-class, user-facing color node: input `index` (int), multi-output `r`/`g`/`b`, config `count` + `entry_<i>_(r|g|b)` + `default_(r|g|b)`. Emits an N-way integer-compare select (discrete lookup; contrast `colorScale` which interpolates). Used by the transform for tag attributes, and available for hand-built graphs. Registered in `MULTI_OUTPUT_TYPES` (compile.ts) + both WASM/WebGPU `VALUE_NODE_EMITTERS` (per-port `setCachedPort` is the multi-output registration). Pure → CSE-eligible by default. Config UI = palette editor in CaNode (`CategoricalColorEditor`). `readCategoricalEntries` / `readCategoricalDefault` are shared by all three emitters.

### Color Scale presets + shared editor
- `src/modeler/vpl/nodes/colorScalePresets.ts` — `COLOR_SCALE_PRESETS` (Grayscale, Viridis, Magma, Plasma, Inferno, Rainbow, Heat, Cool→Warm, Cividis) + `presetStops(name)`. Single source for both consumers.
- `src/modeler/vpl/widgets/GradientStopsEditor.tsx` — the gradient-bar editor (draggable stops + position/color/delete detail + Add Stop) **plus a preset dropdown**, extracted so it's reused by BOTH the Color Scale node (`ColorScaleEditor` is now a thin config↔stops wrapper) AND the linked float/integer editor. The linked editor adds the min/max Range + the Curve (`method`) dropdown for full parity with the node. Bool uses two pickers; tag uses per-option pickers. Defaults: float → Viridis, integer → Rainbow (via the transform's `defaultGradientStops`).

### Cascade ([ModelContext.tsx](src/model/ModelContext.tsx))
- `REMOVE_ATTRIBUTE`: unlinks any mapping linked to the deleted attribute (clears `linked*`).
- `UPDATE_ATTRIBUTE` type change: resets `linkedColors`/`linkedMin`/`linkedMax` (keeps the link) so a stale palette can't mismatch the new type — handled in both the tag/bool branch and the fall-through (covers float↔integer).
- `UPDATE_ATTRIBUTE` tagOptions change: remaps `linkedColors.tag[]` by the same `indexMap` (renamed/reordered keep their color, deleted drop out, new options get `defaultTagColor`).

### Gotchas
- Viewer tabs come from `model.mappings.filter(isAttributeToColor)` ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)), so a linked mapping is selectable with no node placed; the worker dispatches the OM by `mappingId === activeViewer` exactly like a standalone OM (no new runtime path).
- `style={{ width: N, ...sharedStyle }}` foot-gun: if `sharedStyle` sets `width: '100%'`, the spread overrides the `N`. Put the override AFTER the spread (bit the GradientStopsEditor position input — kept the bar from squashing the color/delete controls).
- Inline-style overrides in shared widgets: the position spinbox is fixed-width + `flex: 0 0 auto`; the color input is `flex: 1`. Don't reintroduce `width: 100%` on the spinbox.
- A linked-only model with no Step node still hits the separate "No Step node" compile gate (the empty-graph reorder only covers the `length === 0` check). Realistic models always have a Step; relaxing the Step requirement is out of scope.

---

## 3D Grid CA (milestone, in progress — branch `grid_3d`)

Makes the lattice a **`W×H×D` volume** end-to-end: schema, all three compile targets, 3D neighbourhood editing, authorable RGBA alpha, a WebGL2 voxel renderer, and the save/load + indicators + inspector consumer-scan. Runbook: [docs/HANDOFF_3D_GRID_CA.md](docs/HANDOFF_3D_GRID_CA.md). The engine 3-ification is incremental (`total = W*H*D`, a 3-tuple offset table, a `_layer` decode, **stride stays `coords.length`** so neighbour access is 3D-for-free); the renderer is wholesale new. **`gridDepth===1` / `dimension==='2d'` is the byte-identical 2D fast path on all three targets** (every existing model + legacy file compiles verbatim). Out of scope: bond-graph agents (UI checkbox hard-disabled), variegation-in-3D (2D-locked, V-tab elided in 3D). (The 3-axis `neighborIndex` codec — once out of scope — is now DONE: see PR10.)

### M0a — schema + mode UI (PR1, done)
- **Schema (all additive/optional — old files load unchanged):** `ModelProperties.dimension?: '2d'|'3d'` (absent → `'2d'`), `ModelProperties.gridDepth?: number` (absent → `1`), `CAModel.topologyMode?: { gridCells, agents }` (absent → `{gridCells:true, agents:false}`), `SimulationState.depth?`/`gridDepth?`, `IndicatorXAxis += 'layers'`. **NB the naming collision:** the new checkbox object is `topologyMode`; the legacy `ModelProperties.topology: '2d-grid'` string enum is UNTOUCHED.
- **Migration** ([ModelContext.tsx](src/model/ModelContext.tsx) `LOAD_MODEL`): seeds `dimension`/`gridDepth`/`topologyMode` defaults beside the existing additive guards.
- **Reducer:** `UPDATE_TOPOLOGY_MODE` (clone of `UPDATE_VARIEGATED_CELLS`) rejects an all-false selection (≥1 invariant). `updateTopologyMode` callback is wired into BOTH the context value object AND the useMemo dep array (miss either → stale closure).
- **UI** ([PropertiesPanelContent.tsx](src/modeler/panels/PropertiesPanelContent.tsx)): Dimension radio in the Structure section (3D reveals a Grid Depth `NumberField`); Topology checkboxes in the Execution section above Variegated (Grid Cells UI-disabled when it's the only checked one; Bond-Graph Agents hard-`disabled` + "coming soon"). Mockup: [docs/MOCKUP_PR1_DIMENSION_TOPOLOGY.html](docs/MOCKUP_PR1_DIMENSION_TOPOLOGY.html).
- **B2 (critical):** `gridDepth` + `dimension` are in the `needsFullInit` structural-reinit trigger ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)) — a depth/dimension change must force a full worker reinit (a soft recompile keeps the stale `W*H` buffers + a diverging baked `total`).
- **DEV harness** ([src/dev/compileHarness.ts](src/dev/compileHarness.ts)): `compileAll(model)` compiles on all 3 targets through the real call signatures (`compileGraphWasm` needs `(nodes, edges, model, layout, viewerIds)`; WASM layout via `computeLayoutFromModel`, viewerIds via `buildViewerIds`) and returns `{js.stepCode, wasm.bytesJoined, webgpu.shaderCode}` for the cross-target byte-identity checks. `migrateForHarness` applies the LOAD_MODEL guards to a raw `.gcaproj`. Not imported by production. Use via cache-busted `import('/src/dev/compileHarness.ts?t='+Date.now())` in `preview_eval`.

### PR2+PR3 — JS 3D engine + compiler decode (done; ONE atomic PR per B1)
- **The `is3d` predicate is the single source of truth, shared by worker + compiler:** `is3dModel(model)` (exported from [compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/compile.ts)) = `dimension==='3d' && (gridDepth ?? 1) > 1` (a 1-layer 3D model uses the 2D fast path). The worker derives `depth = dimension==='3d' ? max(1,gridDepth) : 1` and gates on `depth > 1` — the SAME predicate, so a hand-edited `dimension:'2d', gridDepth:8` file can't desync the baked/passed `total`.
- **Worker** ([sim.worker.ts](src/simulator/engine/sim.worker.ts)): `total = W*H*D`; `buildNeighborIndices` gains a `layer` loop + reads 3-tuples (`coords3d` when present, the STRIDE staying `coords.length` so every `nIdx[idx*nSz+k]` consumer is 3D-for-free); `cellIndexOf(layer,row,col)` + `inBounds3d` helpers; the `paint`/`paintManual`/`writeRegion`/`clearRegion` handlers carry an optional `layer` (absent → 0, so 2D is byte-identical). **`buildLoopArgs`/`buildCellArgs` push `depth, WH` ONLY when `depth > 1`** (2D arg list unchanged). `computeMemoryLayout` is called with the 3D `total`, so wasmMemory is correctly 3D-sized even on the JS target (the JS step's typed arrays are views over wasmMemory).
- **Compiler** ([compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/compile.ts)): `decodeCoordLines(is3d, indent)` emits the per-cell preamble — 3D adds `_layer=(idx/WH)|0; _rem=idx-_layer*WH; _row=(_rem/W)|0; _col=_rem-_row*W;`, 2D stays the verbatim `_row=(idx/W)|0; _col=idx-_row*W;` (byte-identical). Applied at all 5 entry points (async step, sync step, InputColor, OutputMapping, InitEvent). `buildLoopParams`/`buildCellParams`/`omParamParts` add `D, WH` **only when `is3d`** (so 2D signatures are byte-identical — the param-list change does NOT happen in 2D). InitEvent emits `_v<id>_z = _layer; _v<id>_maxZ = D-1;` when 3D.
- **InitEvent z/maxZ ports** ([InitEventNode.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/InitEventNode.ts)): added `z`/`maxZ` integer outputs, `hiddenPorts` hides them in 2D (the compiler emits no `_z`/`_maxZ` decode there). This required extending `NodeTypeDef.hiddenPorts` to `(config, model?) => string[]` — threaded the model at both call sites (CaNode + effectivePorts.getEffectivePorts gained an optional `model` param). `initEvent` is already in `MULTI_OUTPUT_TYPES`, so `varName()` resolves z/maxZ via the `_v<id>_<portId>` convention with no extra registration.
- **`withEffectiveNeighborhoods`** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): when a 3D nbr has `includeCentralCell`, appends `[0,0,0]` to `coords3d` AND `[0,0]` to `coords` so the stride invariant holds.
- **`NodeRequirements.lattice2d`** (in [types.ts](src/modeler/vpl/types.ts)) added (PR4 uses it to gate the 2-axis `neighborIndex` family off in 3D; **PR10 un-gated the family** with a 3-axis codec, so no node sets this flag anymore — it remains as generic 2D-only infra).
- **Sample**: [scripts/gen-life3d.mjs](scripts/gen-life3d.mjs) → `public/models/Life3D.gcaproj` — 24³ torus, 26-cell Moore-3D, Bays 5766 (S5-7/B6) via getNeighborsAttribute→aggregate.sum→Compare(between/==)→Logic→setAttribute, InitEvent seeds a randomized central z-slab (uses the `z` port). `useWasm:false` until PR5 ports WASM 3D, then flips to true.
- **Verified**: compiler harness (3D stepCode has the `_layer` decode, `total===W*H*D`; a 2D model's signature has no `D,WH` + verbatim decode → byte-identical); direct compiled-JS-step 0 mismatches vs an independent Bays 5766; and the **real worker** (forced JS) advances generation and matches the independent rule with 0 mismatches over a random fill (validates buildNeighborIndices + the buildLoopArgs↔buildLoopParams ABI).

### PR4 — 3D neighbourhood editing + NI gate (done)
- **Schema**: `Neighborhood.shape?: NeighborhoodShapeSpec` (+ `coords3d?` from PR2). Spec = `{kind:'moore'|'vonNeumann'|'ball'|'rangeN', radius, metric?}` | `{kind:'shell', rIn, rOut}` | `{kind:'ring'|'disk', axis, radius, width?}`. `coords3d` is the runtime source of truth; `coords` stays a same-LENGTH 2D projection (stride invariant).
- **Pure generator** [neighborhood3d.ts](src/modeler/panels/neighborhood3d.ts): `generateCoords3d(spec)` (deterministic layer→row→col scan, excludes origin) + `coords2dProjection` + `describeShape`. Metric: chebyshev=L∞ (Moore), manhattan=L1 (von Neumann), euclidean=L2 (sphere). Unit-verified: vonNeumann r1→6, moore r1→26, ball r2 euclidean→80 (matches independent enum). (TS discriminated-union narrowing on the `'ring'|'disk'` member needs the `'metric' in spec` guard, not control-flow narrowing.)
- **Editor** [Neighborhood3DEditor.tsx](src/modeler/panels/Neighborhood3DEditor.tsx) (rendered by NeighborhoodsPanelContent when `dimension==='3d'`): **Parametric** (shape dropdown + radius/metric/axis/width/rIn/rOut → `generateCoords3d` → updateNeighborhood, clears tags on regen) and **Slice editor** (Z-layer stepper, mark/unmark, the three axis-plane mirrors **H** dc / **V** dr / **L** dl — NOT the 2D D/D2 diagonals; point-toggle + per-cell tags). `applySlice3d` keeps coords/coords3d/tags in lockstep and clears `shape` on hand-edit. The Cell Tags list shows the 3-tuple label in 3D.
- **NI gate (PR4; SUPERSEDED by PR10)**: `NodeRequirements.lattice2d` (in [types.ts](src/modeler/vpl/types.ts)) → hidden in 3D via `isNodeAvailable`/`detectCapabilityRequirements` ([nodeValidation.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/nodeValidation.ts), keyed on `dimension==='3d'`). PR4 set it on the whole `neighborIndex`-value family. **PR10 removed the gate** (3-axis codec) — the whole family now works in 3D, and the `neighborIndex` value-type in Switch/Compare has real sources there. (PR4-era text retained for history; see PR10.)
- **Variegation forced off in 3D**: the Properties variegated checkbox is disabled + the 3D dimension radio dispatches `updateVariegatedCells({enabled:false})`. Mockup: [docs/MOCKUP_PR4_NEIGHBORHOOD3D.html](docs/MOCKUP_PR4_NEIGHBORHOOD3D.html).
- **Verified** (UI via preview_eval): 3D model neighbourhood detail shows Parametric/Slice editor; Generate (Moore r1) → 26 cells; Slice editor renders Z-layer + H/V/L mirrors + grid; all 13 NI nodes `isNodeAvailable===false` in 3D, the safe neighbour nodes true.

### PR5 — WASM 3D port (done; lockstep)
- **Layout** ([wasm/layout.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/wasm/layout.ts)): `computeLayoutFromModel` total = `W*H*depth` (depth honoured only in a 3D model — mirrors the worker). `computeMemoryLayout` keeps its `total` param (the worker already passes `W*H*D` from initGrid). The `neighborhoods` mapper falls back to a `coords3d`-derived 2D list when `coords` is empty (robust nbr-region sizing).
- **Emitter** ([wasm/compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/wasm/compile.ts)): `is3dEntry = dimension==='3d' && gridDepth>1` (per entry). `layerLocal`/`remLocal` allocated ONLY when 3D (so a 2D module's local count + bytes are byte-identical). `emitBody` emits the 3D `layer=idx/WH; rem=idx-layer*WH; row=rem/W; col=rem-row*W` decode when 3D, else the verbatim 2D `row=idx/W; col` two-block. `ctx.layerLocalIdx` (-1 in 2D) feeds the InitEvent `z`/`maxZ` outputs (added to the `initEvent` value emitter, gated on `layerLocalIdx >= 0`). (PR5: `pushNiCellIdx` + the NI emitters were UNTOUCHED/2D-gated; **PR10 made them 3D-aware** — `pushNiCellIdx` + neighborIndexFromOffset/breakDown/flip + getAllNeighborIndexes branch on `layerLocalIdx >= 0`.) The variegated facing emitters stay 2D-gated.
- **Verified**: `computeLayoutFromModel(Life3D).total===13824`, WASM compiles clean; a 2D model's WASM `bytesLen===2157` matches the pre-3D baseline (byte-identical). The **real WASM worker** on Life3D: generation advances 0→1, 3723 cells evolve, **0 mismatches** vs the independent Bays 5766 (JS↔WASM exact parity). Life3D flipped to `useWasm:true`.

### PR6 — WebGPU 3D port (done; lockstep)
- **Layout** ([webgpu/layout.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/webgpu/layout.ts)): `gridDepth` honoured only in a 3D model; `total = W*H*depth`; `WebGPULayout.gridDepth` field. `WebGPULayoutNbr.coords3d?` + the nbr region uses **stride 3** (dRow, dCol, dLayer) in 3D, 2 in 2D (`nbrStride`); `coords3d` falls back to a `dl=0` projection of `coords` for a 2D-only nbr in a 3D model.
- **Encoder** ([webgpu/encoder.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/webgpu/encoder.ts)): `nbrCellIdx` gated on `gd>1` — 3D reads the 3rd offset (`u32(k)*3u + 2u`), decodes `layer=idx/WH; rem; row; col`, wraps/clamps the layer (torus / constant sentinel); 2D emits the verbatim 2-axis helper (byte-identical). (PR6: `nbrCellIdxFromNi` stayed verbatim 2D; **PR10 added its 3D form** — three 10-bit fields resolved via the layer, gated on `gd>1`.) `emitEntryPoint` bound `idx >= ${total}u` is 3D-correct (total = W*H*D).
- **Compiler** ([webgpu/compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/webgpu/compile.ts)): only the **InitEvent** decode is 3D-relevant (the 3 row/col sites at 1196/1983/2077 are the variegated facing emitters — 2D-locked, never reached in 3D). InitEvent emits `y=(idx%WH)/W`, `z=idx/WH`, `maxZ=D-1` when 3D; verbatim 2D otherwise.
- **Runtime** ([webgpuRuntime.ts](src/simulator/engine/webgpuRuntime.ts)): `uploadNeighborOffsets` packs **3 i32/neighbour** when `layout.gridDepth>1` (matches the encoder's `*3u`); 2D packs 2. Dispatch is `Math.ceil(total/64)` — already 3D-correct.
- **Verified**: a 4³ model's `shaderCode` includes `idx >= 64u`, the `*3u` stride, the `layer`/`nl` decode, `dl` read, `init_z`; a 2D model's shader has `*2u`, no layer decode, no 3D dl read (byte-identical structure). The **real WebGPU GPU** on Life3D: generation advances, 3738 cells evolve, **0 mismatches** vs Bays 5766 — **all three targets (JS/WASM/WebGPU) now match exactly**.

### PR7 — RGBA authorable alpha (done; setCellLooks.a + WebGPU alphaMode)
- **Set Cell Looks `a` input port** ([SetCellLooksNode.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/SetCellLooksNode.ts), after `b`, `inlineWidget:'number'`, default `'255'`). `hiddenPorts` hides it with r/g/b in glyph-no-background mode. **JS**: `colors[colorIdx+3] = ${inputs['a'] || '255'}` (colors is Uint8ClampedArray → auto-clamps). **WASM**: `pushValueAs(inputs['a'] ?? 255)` + `i32Store8` (truncates, same as r/g/b). **WebGPU**: default 255 emits the verbatim `(255u << 24u)`; any other input emits `(u32(clamp(<a>,0,255)) << 24u)`. **Default 255 → byte-identical on all 3 targets** (verified: an unchanged setCellLooks model emits `colors[colorIdx+3] = 255;` / `(255u << 24u)` / unchanged WASM; an alpha-128 variant emits `= 128;` / `clamp(128…`).
- **WebGPU canvas** ([webgpuRuntime.ts](src/simulator/engine/webgpuRuntime.ts)): `alphaMode: 'opaque'` → `'premultiplied'`; the present shader writes premultiplied RGB `vec4(r*a, g*a, b*a, a)`. For a=1.0 (default) this is identity, so existing opaque 2D-WebGPU models render unchanged; sub-255 alpha now blends (keeps WebGPU consistent with the JS/WASM 2D canvas, which already composites ImageData alpha source-over).
- **DEV harness** extended: `compileAll(...).js.fullCode` now concatenates step + initCode + all inputColor + all outputMapping code (setCellLooks emits in the OM code, not stepCode).
- **DEFERRED (documented follow-up)**: the Color Scale + Categorical Color `a` OUTPUT channel (for linked-OM / hand-built-gradient alpha). Adding it changes every existing colorScale model's emit unless gated behind an opt-in `hasAlpha` config + the full multi-output registration (varName/MULTI_OUTPUT/setCachedPort × WASM+WebGPU) + per-stop alpha UI. Not needed for the renderer — `setCellLooks.a` is the alpha mechanism (use an explicit Output Mapping with a wired `a` for translucency; a Linked Output Mapping stays opaque). `writeDefaultColors` keeps alpha 255 (correct default); no thumbnail-capture-from-canvas exists, so no opaque-forcing needed there.

### PR8 — WebGL2 3D voxel renderer (done)
- **[src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)** `Gl3DRenderer`: instanced 36-vertex unit cubes; per-instance `[cellIndex, r, g, b, a]` (vertex shader decodes x/y/z from the flat index via `uW`/`uWH`); `uploadColors` compacts ONLY alpha>0 cells (never instances the full volume); orbit camera (yaw/pitch/dist/pan → MVP); clip plane = fragment `discard` past a bounded axis cut (PRIMARY see-inside, NOT depth-sort); opt-in per-cell alpha = GL blend + back-to-front instance sort; colour-id `pick()` via a 2nd FBO pass (idx→RGB, nearest via depth, `readPixels` Y-flipped); `readPixels()` for screenshot/recording. `preserveDrawingBuffer:true`. Tiny self-contained mat4 (perspective/lookAt/mul).
- **SimulatorView seam**: a sibling `<canvas ref={glCanvasRef}>` (the 2D canvas is `display:none` when 3D). `draw()` (useCallback([]), ~20 call sites) branches FIRST on `is3dRef` and reads camera/clip/alpha/depth via refs (`cam3dRef`/`clip3dRef`/`alpha3dRef`/`gridDepth`). Init/dispose effect on `[is3D]`. Pointer effect on the GL canvas: LMB-drag orbit, wheel zoom, MMB/Shift pan; no-drag LMB = paint the picked voxel (radius-1, via `paint3dRef` → the existing paint flush, which now carries `layer`), Shift+click = inspect (`openInspect3dRef` → `commitInspectPopover`). `gridDepth.current` is set beside `gridWidth/Height.current`. Screenshot + recording read the GL display buffer (display resolution — documented semantics change; the 2D recording branch is now an `else if`, skipped in 3D). 3D controls overlay: Reset view, Clip plane (axis X/Y/Z + slider), Alpha blend. DEV hooks: `window.__sim3dCamera/__sim3dClip/__sim3dPick/__sim3dRenderer/__sim3dRedraw`.
- **WebGPU in 3D** (updated, feedback round 1): WebGPU is now a selectable compile target for 3D. The earlier "force WASM in 3D" was because WebGPU **direct-render** writes to an OffscreenCanvas and skips the CPU colors readback the WebGL renderer needs — so in 3D the canvas-attach is gated OFF (`pendingCanvasAttach` requires `!is3D`), which keeps WebGPU on the **readback** path (colors shipped via `stepped`). Net: WebGPU **compute** runs the simulation, the worker reads colours back each step, and the WebGL2 voxel renderer draws them. Verified: WebGPU-3D Life3D ships 4195 instances + matches Bays 5766 (0 mismatches). (The LOAD_MODEL force-WASM migration + the disabled WebGPU radio were removed.)
- **Verified** (readPixels-based per the handoff — preview_screenshot times out on heavy pages): Life3D loads → renderer inits; `instanceCount` = alive-cell count (culling); orbit changes the rendered pixels; clip plane reduces visible pixels then restores; `pick(centre)` returns a valid cell, `pick(corner)` returns -1; the worker 3D `paint` sets exactly the target voxel; 3D controls + GL-canvas-visible / 2D-hidden confirmed.
- **Brush shape on the plane (feedback round 2)**: the 3D brush now **stamps the selected brush shape** (Rect / Circle / Ring + size, Line → its width-dot) onto the interaction plane, like the 2D brush — no longer a single voxel. `paint3dRef(layer,row,col)` maps the 2D stamp offsets `(dRow, dCol)` onto the plane's two FREE grid axes (z-plane → row+col, identical to 2D; y-plane → layer+col; x-plane → layer+row), torus-wraps the free axes (bounded models clip via the worker's `inBounds3d`), and **Bresenham-interpolates** between successive picks during a drag (`last3dHitRef`, reset on pointer down/up) so fast strokes leave no gaps. It pushes the full cell list to the existing `paint` / `paintManual` worker handlers (which already take per-cell `layer`), and — like the 2D `paintAt` — **coalesces via the shared `pendingPaintRaf`/`flushPaintBatch` rAF batcher** (one worker round-trip per frame, NOT per pointermove) with a synchronous `flushPaintBatch()` on pointer-up so a click/quick-release still commits (an adversarial review flagged the original per-move synchronous flush as a worker-flooding regression). DEV hook `window.__sim3dPaint(layer,row,col,reset?)`. Verified via worker message capture: z/y/x planes each lay the disc flat in the correct slice, a drag interpolates, and the stamp wraps on the torus. (Still single-cell on the colour-id `pick()` inspect path — that's a point query, not a brush.) Mockup: [docs/MOCKUP_PR8_VOXEL_RENDERER.html](docs/MOCKUP_PR8_VOXEL_RENDERER.html).
- **Follow-up (DONE — feedback round 4)**: the cursor now outlines the **full brush footprint** (every affected cell), not just the centre — see "3D viewport — feedback round 4" below.

### 3D viewport — feedback round 3 (RMB pan, clickable gizmo POV, brush-plane indicator, hover cursor)
- **RMB pans** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) pointer effect): RMB (`button === 2`) is otherwise unused in 3D, so a right-drag now pans (in addition to Shift+MMB / Shift+Alt+LMB). The existing `onCtx` `preventDefault` suppresses the context menu. The gizmo-click check is LMB-only, so RMB/MMB still orbit/pan.
- **Clickable corner gizmo → 6 POVs** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the gizmo now draws all **6 ± axis stubs + endpoint dots** (`GIZMO_ENDS` table; `gl_PointSize` via a new `uPointSize` uniform on `LINE_VS`; shared `gizmoMatrix()`/`gizmoSizePx()`/`drawLines()`). `gizmoHitTest(cssX,cssY,cssW,cssH)` maps a click (CSS→device px, bottom-left gizmo viewport at `(10,10)+S`) to the nearest projected endpoint `{axis, sign}` (NDC threshold 0.55, facing-the-camera tie-break for edge-on endpoints), or null outside. `onDown` checks it FIRST (LMB only, before any drag/paint) and `setPov(axis,sign)` snaps the camera: x→yaw 0/π, y→yaw ±π/2 (pitch 0); z→pitch ±1.49 keeping yaw (±1.49 stays shy of the `cameraBasis` pole degeneracy at ±π/2). Snaps instantly (no tween).
- **Brush-plane bounds + grid** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderBrushPlane()`): when the Brush plane is enabled, the renderer draws a bright cyan bounded rectangle + an interior grid ON the slice (depth-tested, so voxels in front occlude it) at the plane's world coordinate — `z = hz - pos` (z-plane), `y = pos - hy` (y), `x = pos - hx` (x), matching `pickOnPlane`'s mapping exactly so the drawn plane is precisely where the brush paints. `draw()` feeds it via `r.setBrushPlane(enabled ? {axis,pos} : null)`.
- **Hover cube cursor** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): an amber wireframe cube (12 edges, depth-test OFF so it's always visible like a cursor) outlines the hovered cell on the brush plane. SimulatorView's `onMove` calls `updateHover()` (= `pickOnPlane` at the cursor) on idle moves AND during a brush drag, **redrawing only when the hovered cell CHANGES** (a full GL re-render per raw pointermove is wasteful on large volumes); `onLeave` clears it. (**Round 4 generalised this to the full brush FOOTPRINT** — `setHoverCells`/`renderHoverCells` — see "feedback round 4". **Perf round** added the colors-upload gate so a re-render no longer re-scans the volume — see "3D perf — feedback round 5".)
- **Verified** (runtime): all 6 gizmo endpoints hit-test in-region (null outside); the 4 POVs snap to the expected yaw/pitch (x+→0/0, y+→π/2/0, z±→±1.49 keeping yaw) via a synthetic gizmo click; `renderBrushPlane` (+~43k px) and `renderHoverCube` (+~430 px, also without the plane) both add rendered pixels; an RMB-drag moved the camera target. Build + tsc clean.

### 3D viewport — feedback round 4 (footprint cursor, 3D line, default transparent, shift-inspect, bg colour, depth resize, focus)
- **Default voxel colours are TRANSPARENT in 3D** ([sim.worker.ts](src/simulator/engine/sim.worker.ts) `writeDefaultColors`): when `depth > 1` the fallback fills alpha 0 (`colors.fill(0)`) so the voxel renderer instances ZERO cells — a model with no Output Mapping (or before its first colour pass) no longer builds a full opaque volume on every recompile that the sim would erase. 2D keeps the visible first-bool fallback.
- **Footprint cursor** (the round-3 single cube → the whole brush shape): renderer `setHoverCell`→**`setHoverCells(cells[])`** + `renderHoverCube`→**`renderHoverCells`** (a wireframe cube per affected cell). SimulatorView's `updateHover` computes the FOOTPRINT into `hoverCells3dRef` via `footprintFor(hit)` (the line preview when a Line anchor is staged, else `mapStampToPlane(hit, currentStampOffsets())`); `draw()` feeds `r.setHoverCells(...)`. Redraw-on-change is kept, but a staged Line always recomputes (the preview grows with the cursor).
- **`mapStampToPlane(center, offsets)`** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): the SINGLE source of truth that maps 2D stamp offsets onto the plane's free axes (+ torus wrap) — used by `paint3dRef`'s `stampAt` (refactored to call it), the hover footprint, and the line. So the highlighted cursor is exactly what gets painted.
- **3D Line tool** (was: clicking acted like a circle + immediate apply): `line3dAnchorRef` two-click staging in `onDown` (first plain-LMB stages the plane-cell anchor, no paint; second commits `paintLine3d` = `lineFootprint` (2D `lineStampCells` in the plane's free coords → `mapStampToPlane`)). RMB / Escape / brush-shape / brush-mapping / brush-plane change all clear the anchor. No drag-paint in Line mode.
- **Shift+LMB inspects** (was: Ctrl/Cmd, and plain Shift+LMB painted): inspect was `active='inspect'` on `e.button===0 && (shiftKey||ctrlKey||metaKey) && !alt`; the colour-id `pick()` fires on a no-move pointer-up. Plain LMB still brushes; Shift+Alt/MMB still pan. (**Round 6 narrows this**: Ctrl/Cmd+LMB now RESIZES the brush, so ONLY Shift+LMB inspects — see "feedback round 6".)
- **Canvas click restores keyboard focus** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) `onDown`): blurs a focused `INPUT/TEXTAREA/SELECT` before `preventDefault`, so the transport shortcuts (Enter/Space/Esc) work after clicking the 3D canvas (the canvas isn't focusable; without the blur the last-edited field kept focus and the shortcut handler bailed).
- **Background colour** (3D View panel): `bg3d {enabled,color}` → `bg3dRef [r,g,b,a]|null` → renderer `setBackgroundColor` (used in `render()`'s `gl.clearColor`). Default transparent (page shows through); a checkbox + colour input fills it opaquely.
- **Depth in the simulator resize** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): `simDepth` state + a Depth `NumberField` shown in the Grid Dimensions panel only when 3D. `initWorkerWithDimensions(w, h, dOverride?)` honours the override (bakes `gridDepth` + `dimension` into the compiler `dimsModel` so WebGPU's baked `total` + the 3D codec match), unifies `d3` across the pan/zoom + init-message paths, and `setSimDepth(d3)`; 2D (no override) is unchanged.
- **Verified** (runtime): canvas click blurs the focused input (activeElement INPUT→BODY); bg red applies + transparent restores + the Background control/colour input exist; the footprint cursor renders a radius-4 disc (38280 amber px); the Line tool stages on the first plane click (0 paints) and commits a 160-cell capsule on the second; Shift+LMB does not paint; the Depth field shows in 3D; Life3D loads 24³. tsc + build clean.

### 3D perf — feedback round 5 (camera-only redraws skip the full-volume upload)
- **The win:** `draw()`'s 3D branch used to call `r.uploadColors(colors3d, total)` on EVERY redraw — orbit, pan, wheel, auto-orbit (rAF), hover, clip/viz toggles — re-scanning all `total` cells for alpha>0 and re-uploading the compacted instance buffer, even when only the CAMERA moved and the colours were unchanged. At 100³ that's ~1e6 cells scanned + a GPU upload PER FRAME of orbiting.
- **The gate** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): the worker hands a FRESH colours buffer on every `stepped` (`colorsRef.current = msg.colors`; never mutated in place — verified), so buffer IDENTITY changes iff colours changed. `draw()` now uploads only when `colors3d !== lastUploadedColors3dRef.current` (then stamps the ref); camera-only frames reuse the existing GPU instance buffer and just re-render. `lastUploadedColors3dRef` is reset to `null` when the renderer is recreated (the `[is3D]` init effect) and when the colours buffer is cleared on reinit (`initWorkerWithDimensions`), so a fresh renderer / resize always re-uploads. 2D is untouched (the gate is inside the 3D branch).
- **Verified** (runtime): 6 camera-only redraws (yaw changes) → **0** `uploadColors` calls; one simulation step → **1**. tsc + build clean.
- **NOT low-hanging (noted, not done):** the JS/WASM `buildNeighborIndices` precomputed per-cell neighbour table is `total × nSz` Int32 (≈104 MB at 100³ Moore; scales multiplicatively in 3D) — the dominant 3D memory cost. Avoiding it means inline neighbour computation like the WebGPU path (which already uses the compact offset table per §2.1), a substantial engine change that could also slow per-step JS/WASM. Out of scope for a "low-hanging" pass. Minor leftovers (per-frame `new Float32Array` for the static gizmo/overlay line buffers; the alpha-blend `sortBackToFront` keys/order/sorted allocation) are small and were left as-is.

### 3D viewport — feedback round 6 (inspect highlight not line, volumetric brush, ctrl-resize + ctrl-wheel, gizmo depth/letters, axes at origin)
- **Inspect cell HIGHLIGHT replaces the 2D connector line in 3D** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) + [gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the dotted-yellow `InspectHoverLink` (whose `gridToScreen` projection is 2D-only and can't track a 3D voxel) is gated `!is3D` at BOTH render sites (the pinned-popover hover link + the sweep link). Instead, hovering an inspect popover highlights its cell IN the volume: a `useEffect` keyed on `hoveredInspectIdx` decodes the flat idx → `{layer,row,col}` into `inspectHighlight3dRef`; `draw()`'s 3D branch calls `r.setInspectCells(inspectHighlight3dRef.current)` every frame; the renderer draws them as **white** wireframe cubes via `renderHoverCells` — the same depth-test-OFF always-on-top path as the amber hover-footprint cursor, refactored into a shared `pushCellCube(out, c, col, h)` (inspect uses `h=0.6`, hover `0.56`). Hover-leave clears the ref → highlight vanishes. (NB for verification: `draw()` re-sets `setInspectCells(inspectHighlight3dRef.current)` every frame, so a manual `r.setInspectCells([...])` + `__sim3dRedraw()` is immediately overwritten — drive it through the popover's real `onMouseEnter`/`onMouseLeave`.) **2D inspector unchanged** (still draws the connector line + cell outline).
- **Volumetric brush — "Extrapolate plane (volumetric brush)" checkbox** (rendered ONLY when `is3D`, in the brush controls): `brush3dVolume` state + `brush3dVolumeRef` (synced via effect) + persisted in `genesisca_sim_settings` (object + dep array). OFF = the flat plane footprint (historical). ON = the brush shape becomes a 3D SOLID: `brushShapeOffsets3d(shape,…)` returns 3-tuples `[dr,dc,dl]` — circle→**sphere**, ring→**spherical shell**, rect→**box** (`bw col × bh row × bh layer`), line→a **ball** preview-dot. `currentStampOffsets3d()` (cached by a `v|shape|…` key) returns them in volume mode, else falls back to `currentStampOffsets()` (2-tuples). `mapStampToPlane` was widened to `(center, offsets: ReadonlyArray<readonly number[]>)` and now reads an optional 3rd `dl`, applying it to the plane's **FIXED** axis (z→layer, y→row, x→col), torus-wrapped — so `dl=0` (every 2D/flat call, where offsets are 2-tuples) is byte-identical to before. `paint3dRef`'s `stampAt` + the hover `footprintFor` both call `currentStampOffsets3d()`; `lineFootprint` extrudes the capsule along the fixed axis by `±⌊width/2⌋` in volume mode. Verified: a radius-2 circle paints an 81-cell sphere across 5 layers vs a 21-cell disc on 1 layer. (**Round 7** renames the checkbox to **"Volumetric Brush"** and adds a separate box **Depth** field — `boxDepth` param on `brushShapeOffsets3d`.)
- **Ctrl+LMB = resize, Shift+LMB = inspect, Ctrl+wheel ≠ zoom** (3D pointer effect — SUPERSEDES round 4's `(shift||ctrl||meta)`-inspect): `onDown` now routes `button 0 && (ctrl||meta)` → `active='resize'` (snapshots `resizeStart` = current w/h/radius/ringW/lineW) and `button 0 && shift` → `active='inspect'`. `onMove`'s new `resize` branch mirrors the 2D Ctrl-drag math (`±round(totDx/5)` primary size, `−round(totDy/5)` secondary, per active shape, clamped) → `setBrushRadius`/`setBrushRingWidth`/`setBrushLineWidth`/`setBrushW`+`setBrushH`, then `updateHover`+`draw`. `onWheel` early-returns on `ctrl||meta` (that combo cycles Input Mappings elsewhere) so it no longer ALSO zooms; plain wheel still zooms. Verified: Ctrl+drag grows the radius (2→14, no inspect popover); Ctrl+wheel leaves `cam.dist` unchanged (1.9→1.9), plain wheel changes it (1.9→2.09).
- **Gizmo gains DEPTH + axis LETTERS** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderGizmo`): the corner widget used to draw all 6 axis arms with no depth test, so a back arm (e.g. −X) could paint OVER a front one. Now it **scissor-clears only the gizmo viewport's depth** (`enable(SCISSOR_TEST)` + `scissor(…)` + `clear(DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT)` + `disable(SCISSOR_TEST)` + `enable(DEPTH_TEST)`) and draws the axes + endpoint points with **DEPTH TEST ON** (front occludes back), then draws the axis **letters** (C=col/+X, R=row/+Y, D=depth/+Z — `static GIZMO_LABELS` + stroke `static GLYPHS`) with depth OFF, an identity MVP, projecting each positive tip to NDC (×1.22 outward) and **skipping back-facing letters** (`facing < −0.15`). GL state (scissor/depth/viewport) is restored so subsequent passes are unaffected. (**Round 7** reworks the letters — drops the back-face cull, uses a constant radial offset + uniform size — and updates the label dirs to **C→+X, R→-Y, D→-Z**; the depth-correct axis stub drawing here is unchanged.)
- **Main Axes drawn from the ORIGIN, not the volume centre** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderOverlays`, the `if (this.viz.axes)` block): the toggleable RGB axes now start at cell `(0,0,0)`'s world centre (`-hx, -hy, +hz` in the Z-up remap, since layer 0 sits at the top) and grow toward **+col** (red, `+x`), **+row** (green, `+y`), **+depth** (blue, downward `−z`, because layer increases downward), each capped with a 2-pronged arrowhead — so they mark WHERE the origin is and WHICH WAY row/col/depth increase, instead of crossing through the middle of the volume. (**Round 7** flips **+row → -Y** so the top view matches the 2D CA — the origin is now at `(-hx, +hy, +hz)` and +row grows toward -Y/screen-down.)
- **Verified** (runtime via `window.__sim3d*` hooks + readPixels + screenshot): inspect popover opens in 3D and hovering it highlights the cell (2157-byte frame diff) / leaving clears it (0-byte); volumetric sphere 81 cells across 5 layers vs flat disc 21/1; Ctrl-drag radius 2→14 with 0 inspect popovers; Ctrl+wheel dist unchanged, plain wheel zooms; Axes + Gizmo toggles render with no GL/console errors; the origin axes (red/green/blue from the corner) + Z-up gizmo render as intended in the screenshot. tsc + build clean. 2D paint/inspect paths byte-identical (dl defaults 0; the new effect + checkbox are 3D-gated).

### 3D viewport — feedback round 7 (top view = 2D orientation, depth-POV roll, gizmo letters, sweep inspect, "Volumetric Brush" + box depth)
- **Top-down view now matches the 2D CA — row axis FLIPPED to world -Y** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the world remap is now **col→+X (right), row→-Y (DOWN the screen), depth/layer→-Z (into the screen, layer 0 on top)**. A camera can't mirror, so getting col-right AND row-down in the top view required flipping the ROW axis from +Y to -Y (`world_y = halfY - row`). EVERY world-remap site flips row consistently: the `VS` + `PICK_VS` shader centres (`uHalf.y - row`), `pushCellCube` / `sortBackToFront` (`cy = hy - row`), `pickOnPlane` (the `'y'` plane = `hy - pos`, inverse `row = round(hy - wy)`), `renderOverlays` axes (origin `oy = +hy`; +row → -Y), `renderBrushPlane` (`'y'` plane `y = hy - pos`). **SUPERSEDES round 6's "+row → +Y" axes line above.** Renderer-only → **ZERO 2D impact** (2D uses the Canvas2D path). Verified: top-view `pickOnPlane` at screen-centre / +80px-right / +80px-down → col increases right, row increases down, all on layer 0.
- **Camera roll at the ±Depth POV** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `setCamera` + [SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) `setPov`): looking down/up the Z (depth) axis makes WORLD_UP (+Z) parallel to the view → `lookAt` degenerate. `setCamera` swaps in **up = `[0, fwd.z>0 ? -1 : 1, 0]`** when `|camForward.z| > 0.999` (top → up +Y → col-right/row-down; bottom → up -Y), else WORLD_UP. `setPov` sets the depth POV pitch to **±π/2 EXACTLY** (was ±1.49) so the snap reaches the pole-up branch; the general orbit clamps `|pitch| ≤ 1.5` (`|fwd.z| ≤ 0.997 < 0.999`) so orbiting KEEPS WORLD_UP — the Y-up only kicks in at the exact snap (a deliberate roll "pop" between the snapped top and the first orbit frame, accepted). `setPov` also changed the x/y POVs to **look ALONG the clicked axis tip** (`dir = -tipV`), so clicking the **D** gizmo tip (at -Z = the +depth direction) gives the **top / 2D-matching** view.
- **Gizmo letters reworked** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderGizmo` + `GIZMO_LABELS`): label directions follow the new orientation (**C→+X, R→-Y, D→-Z**). The letter rendering **dropped the back-face cull** (that was the "letters disappear at some angles" bug — all 3 now always draw), nudges each glyph a **constant radial offset** beyond its tip (fixes off-centre), and uses a **uniform NDC size** (`sz 0.26`, `pushOut 0.24`). Verified: label-tint pixels present in the gizmo region at multiple camera angles (no disappearing).
- **3D sweep inspect — Shift+LMB DRAG** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) `sweepPick3d` + the 3D pointer effect): holding Shift+LMB and dragging shows a **single transient popover** (reuses the 2D `sweepInspector` state) that tracks the **front voxel** under the cursor (colour-id `pick()`), refreshes its data live, and **highlights** the cell (`inspectHighlight3dRef`) — quick region investigation without pinning a popover per cell. Release **without** moving PINS (the round-6 `openInspect3dRef` path); release **after** a drag DISCARDS (mirrors the 2D sweep inspector's `!moved` rule). The popover anchors at the press point (no 2D connector line in 3D). The round-6 hover-highlight effect (keyed on `hoveredInspectIdx`) doesn't run during a sweep (that idx stays null), so the two don't fight; the sweep clears the highlight on release. 2D + 3D are mutually exclusive (the 3D pointer effect early-returns `if (!is3D)`; 2D handlers live on the `display:none` 2D canvas), so sharing the sweep refs is safe. Verified: down → 1 popover, drag → follows (cell updates, stays 1), release-after-drag → 0.
- **"Volumetric Brush" (renamed) + box DEPTH field** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): round 6's "Extrapolate plane (volumetric brush)" checkbox is renamed **"Volumetric Brush"**. The volumetric **box** (rect shape) gained its own **Depth** field (`brushBoxDepth` state/ref/sync-effect/persist; shown when `is3D && brush3dVolume && rect`, max `gridDepth.current||simDepth`) so the box's LAYER extent is independent of the row size (H): `brushShapeOffsets3d` takes a `boxDepth` param (`halfD = ⌊(boxDepth-1)/2⌋`, same off-centre bias as W/H), threaded through `currentStampOffsets3d` (+ the stamp cache key `v|shape|bw|bh|radius|ringW|boxD`). Sphere/shell/tube unchanged. The box-depth rides the SAME volumetric paint pipeline already runtime-verified for the sphere.
- **Verified**: `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; top-view orientation, gizmo letters, and sweep inspect runtime-confirmed; the Depth field appears for rect+Volumetric; 2D paths untouched (all new behaviour is 3D-gated / renderer-only).

### Post-PR8 — camera/viewport overhaul + interaction-plane brush (feedback round 1)
- **Z-up Blender camera** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the world remap is now **Z-up** — XY is the horizon (the 2D-CA plane), `z = uHalf.z - layer` (layer increases DOWNWARD), so a top-down view shows the grid like a 2D CA. `Camera3D` = `{ yaw, pitch, dist, target:[x,y,z] }` (target replaces panX/panY). `cameraBasis()` derives forward/right/up; **`panCamera(cam, dx, dy, scale)`** (exported) pans the target along the camera's right/up axes (screen-space pan, tracks the viewpoint). Input scheme (SimulatorView pointer effect): **MMB-drag OR Alt+LMB-drag = orbit**, **Shift+(MMB/Alt+LMB) = pan**, **wheel = zoom**, **plain LMB = brush** (interaction plane), **Ctrl/Cmd+LMB click = inspect**. (LMB is the brush, so orbit is on MMB/Alt+LMB, Blender-style. **Round 3 adds RMB-drag = pan.**)
- **Scene overlays + gizmo** (`Viz3D = {axes, grid, bounds, gizmo}`): a 2nd unlit line program draws toggleable RGB **axes**, an XY floor **grid** (stepped if >100 cells/axis), and the volume **bounds** box; a corner **gizmo** viewport shows the axes rotating with the camera (orthographic, `camDir`). `setViz()` per frame from `viz3dRef`. (**Round 3 makes the gizmo clickable** — 6 ± endpoints snap to the main POVs — see "feedback round 3".)
- **Clip plane gained a `'camera'` axis** — discards fragments past a plane perpendicular to the view direction (`uClipForward` = camForward); peels toward the viewer. X/Y/Z unchanged.
- **Interaction-plane brush** (the 3D brush): `pickOnPlane(cssX, cssY, cssW, cssH, axis, pos)` unprojects the cursor through `inv(MVP)` to a world ray and intersects the axis-aligned slice plane at grid index `pos`, returning the nearest in-bounds `{layer,row,col}` (or null). LMB-drag ray-traces onto the plane → `paint3dRef`, which **stamps the full brush shape on the plane + interpolates the drag** (see PR8 "Brush shape on the plane"). Controlled by the **Brush plane** toggle (axis + position slider). `mat4Invert`/`unproject`/`mat4Ortho` added.
- **Auto-orbit** (`orbit3d {on, speed}`): a rAF loop spins `cam.yaw` while enabled + 3D + visible. **Collapsible 3D View panel** (`controls3dOpen`) — Reset view, Axes/Grid/Bounds/Gizmo toggles, Auto-orbit+speed, Clip plane (X/Y/Z/View + slider), Brush plane (axis + slider), Alpha blend.
- **Verified** (readPixels): orbit/pan change the image; axes/grid/bounds/gizmo add pixels; camera-axis clip reduces pixels; `pickOnPlane` resolves the correct cell for top-down + 3/4 views on X/Y/Z planes (passing the drawing-buffer dims — the GL canvas's CSS layout size can read 0 in a headless eval, but the real pointer handler uses `getBoundingClientRect`).
- **Live 3D neighbourhood preview** ([NeighborhoodPreview3D.tsx](src/modeler/panels/NeighborhoodPreview3D.tsx)): a small orbiting canvas in the 3D neighbourhood editor that reuses `Gl3DRenderer` to show the centre cell (amber) + neighbour offsets (green) inside a bounds box (auto-orbit + drag + wheel). Rebuilds a synthetic `(2·maxAbs+1)³` colours buffer on `coords3d` change. Verified: the preview canvas lays out + renders (295×170, GL context constructs clean), Generate (Moore r1) → 26 cells reflected.

### PR9 — save/load + indicators + inspector 3D consumer-scan (done)
- **Save/load** ([fileOperations.ts](src/model/fileOperations.ts)): the worker getState payload echoes `depth` ([sim.worker.ts](src/simulator/engine/sim.worker.ts)); `serializeSimState`/`serializePreset` write `gridDepth` (always) + `depth` (inside the grid block); `readStateFile` normalizes `depth`/`gridDepth` → 1 for legacy files. `applySimulationState` deserializes at `total = W*H*depth` (a 3D state would otherwise truncate to its first layer), threads `depth` through `dimsFromState`/`dimsChanged` (adapting the model + flipping it to `dimension:'3d'` when `depth>1`), and the hard dim-validation rejects a depth mismatch with a `W×H×D` message (3rd dim shown only when either side is a volume). The preset-load dim check mirrors it. **`gridDepth.current`** is set beside `gridWidth/Height.current` (init + applySimulationState).
- **Spatial `'layers'` axis**: `computeSpatialIndicators` ([sim.worker.ts](src/simulator/engine/sim.worker.ts)) accepts `xAxis==='layers'` (axisLen = depth; the posBin decode is now 3D-correct — layer=⌊i/WH⌋, row/col within the layer — behaviour-identical in 2D); the `hasSpatialIndicators` gate + the post-loop linked-skip + the WebGPU-reduction-skip all include `'layers'`. `IndicatorSpatialChart` widens `axis` to `'layers'` (axisName 'layer'); `IndicatorDisplay` gained a `gridDepth` prop (axisLength = depth for layers) + its three `isSpatial` checks include `'layers'`; the IndicatorsPanelSection X-axis dropdown offers **Layers (spatial, 3D)** only when `dimension==='3d'`. **Verified**: a per-layer frequency indicator's `true` series matches an independent re-bin by ⌊i/WH⌋ with 0 mismatches.
- **Inspector** ([InspectCellPopover.tsx](src/simulator/InspectCellPopover.tsx)): the `neighborIndex` value decode was 2-axis `(dr, dc)` in PR9; **PR10 made it dimension-aware** — 3D models decode `(dr, dc, dl)` via `unpackNI3` (an `is3d` prop threaded from SimulatorView).
- **Verified**: 3D getState `depth=24`; serialized `gridDepth/depth=24` + the `alive` buffer base64-decodes to the full 13824 bytes (not truncated to W*H); spatial-layers 0 mismatches. `ATTR_TYPE_MAP` needs no change (a longer buffer base64's automatically).

### PR10 — 3-axis NeighborIndex offsets + un-gate the NI family in 3D (done; lockstep)
- **Why:** PR4 gated the whole `neighborIndex` family off in 3D because the packed codec carried only two 16-bit `(dr, dc)` halves. PR10 adds a 3-axis codec and un-gates the family, so offset-based neighbour access (the imperative `getAllNeighborIndexes → filter/pick/forEach → get/setNeighborAttributeByIndex` style) works on the 3D lattice. User decision: "enable 3D offsets, defer the rename" (the `neighborIndex` type id + node labels are unchanged; only 3D support added).
- **Codec** ([niCodec.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/niCodec.ts)): 3D packs three sign-extended **10-bit** fields (`dr<<20 | dc<<10 | dl`, ±511/axis); 2D keeps the verbatim 16-bit codec → **2D output byte-identical**. `INVALID_NI = 0x80000000` stays valid in BOTH (bit 31 free in 3D's bits 0-29; consumers compare the bit pattern before decoding). New: `packNI3`/`unpackNI3`; the emit helpers `niDrExpr`/`niDcExpr`(+`is3d`) / `niDlExpr` / `niPackExpr`(+`is3d`,`dlExpr`) / `niCellExprStmts`(+`is3d` → a 3D branch using `_layer`/`D`/`WH`). `is3dModelLike(model)` — a lightweight 3D check for node files (avoids importing `is3dModel` from compile.ts → circular).
- **Per-model codec selection (single source of truth per target):** JS = `CompileContext.is3d` (= `is3dModel(model)`), threaded into every NI node's `compile(...ctx)`. WASM = `ctx.layerLocalIdx >= 0`. WebGPU = `ctx.layout.gridDepth > 1`. All three resolve the cell via the layer (`(nl*H+nr)*W+nc`, torus wrap or constant→`total` sentinel).
- **JS** ([compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/compile.ts)): pre-pass `packCoord(nbr, slot)` packs from `coords3d`+`packNI3` in 3D (for getAllNeighborIndexes / getNeighborIndexesByTags / neighborIndexFromTag); `niCellExprStmts` callers (get/getNeighborsAttr/filter/set/markCellUpdated/moveSelfToNeighbor) pass `ctx?.is3d`; `neighborIndexFromOffset` / `breakDownNeighborIndex` / `flipNeighborIndex` use the dim-aware helpers.
- **WASM** ([wasm/compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/wasm/compile.ts)): `pushNiCellIdx` 3D branch; `neighborIndexFromOffset`/`breakDown`/`flip` + `getAllNeighborIndexes` emit the 10-bit codec; the tag pre-pass now packs `_resolvedTagIndexes`/`_resolvedPacked` 3D-aware (this also **fixed a latent bug**: the WASM-only path emitted bare *slot indices* instead of packed NIs — masked whenever the JS pre-pass resolved the config first).
- **WebGPU** ([webgpu/encoder.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/webgpu/encoder.ts) + [compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/webgpu/compile.ts)): `nbrCellIdxFromNi` 3D form in the encoder; same three node emitters + `getAllNeighborIndexes` 3D. (Tag NIs read the JS/WASM-resolved packed config, as before.)
- **Un-gate**: removed `requirements.lattice2d` from all 13 NI nodes; the flag is kept as generic 2D-only infra (no current users — comment updated in types.ts). `neighborIndexFromOffset` + `breakDownNeighborIndex` gain a `dl` port, hidden in 2D via `hiddenPorts(_config, model) => is3dModelLike(model) ? [] : ['dl']`. `breakDownNeighborIndex`'s `dl` resolves via the multi-output `_v<id>_dl` convention (already in MULTI_OUTPUT_TYPES); `flipNeighborIndex` preserves dl (the flips are XY-plane). The `neighborIndex` value-type in Switch/Compare needs no change — it compares the packed bit pattern (dimension-agnostic) and now has real sources in 3D.
- **UI**: `NeighborIndexValuePicker` + `NeighborIndexDefaultEditor` ([NeighborIndexDefaultEditor.tsx](src/modeler/panels/NeighborIndexDefaultEditor.tsx)) take an `is3d` prop → pack/decode 3 axes + a `dl` (layer) stepper, grid hint-membership per layer (`coords3d`), 10-bit clamp (±511). Threaded from AttributesPanelContent (default/boundary/undefined editors), ManualBrushPanel, the simulator model-attr panel, and the cell inspector (`unpackNI3`) — all keyed on `dimension==='3d' && (gridDepth??1)>1`.
- **Verified**: tsc + full build clean; cross-target compile parity via the harness (all three emit the 10-bit codec; 2D byte-identical); JS direct-runtime 0 mismatches on a layer-up shift; and the **real worker on all three targets** — a "copy the cell one layer below" rule (`neighborIndexFromOffset(0,0,-1) → getNeighborAttributeByIndex`) rose a seeded ring exactly one layer per step on WASM (L0→L3), JS (L0→L5), and WebGPU (L0→L4), confirming cross-target 3D-NI runtime parity. (This was driven by a throwaway `Shift3D` demo model + a `gen-shift3d.mjs` generator — both **removed** afterward as too specific to ship in the Models Library; the NI-3D code is regression-covered by the dev harness, which needs no shipped model.)

## Reroute Links (wire reroute points)

Movable relay dots placed on a wire (Blender / Unreal blueprint style) so users can bend connections around nodes and fan one output out to many consumers without long crossing wires. Two layers, deliberately decoupled.

### Editor layer (visual-only node)
- React Flow `type: 'rerouteNode'` (in the `nodeTypes` map), rendered by [RerouteNodeComponent.tsx](src/modeler/vpl/RerouteNodeComponent.tsx) — a tiny ~16px dot. It is **NOT** a registry `NodeTypeDef` (like comment / group nodes); it has no `compile()` and never reaches a compiler.
- `data: { nodeType: 'reroute', portCategory: 'value'|'flow', dataType?, config: {} }`. `portCategory` is **locked at creation** from the relayed output (flow ≠ value). One input handle `input_<cat>_in` (left) + one output handle `output_<cat>_out` (right); colored by category (value cyan / flow green / NI amber), with the same compatible/incompatible glow as CaNode handles (subscribes to `connectingFrom`).
- **A reroute always relays an OUTPUT, never an input** → exactly one inbound edge, any number of outbound. So it's a single-input / multi-output relay. **Composes freely:** many reroutes per output port (ordinary fan-out) and chains of reroutes on one link (`A → R1 → R2 → R3 → B`). **Single-incoming is enforced** in `isValidConnection`: a reroute's input rejects a second incoming wire (for BOTH value and flow — a reroute is not a merge point; the collapse pass would otherwise silently keep only the first inbound). `collapseReroutes` keeps "first inbound wins" purely as a defensive fallback for hand-edited files.
- **Normal draggable node:** reroutes are NOT `draggable: false`. They move individually AND as part of a multi-selection (drag a selection of nodes and reroutes go with it) — the earlier `draggable: false` approach broke selection moves and was removed. The press-and-hold gesture is ONLY for creating a reroute on a wire, never for moving an existing one (moving is a plain drag).
- Serialization round-trips like comment/group: `toRFNodes`/`toGraphNodes` map the `rerouteNode` type (the shared type-mapping ternary in [GraphEditor.tsx](src/modeler/vpl/GraphEditor.tsx) was extended at all 4 sites: load, paste, duplicate, undo-macro). `nodeSize` has a 16px case. No schema/`types.ts` change (GraphNode/GraphEdge already round-trip arbitrary node types).

### Creation (press-and-hold gesture, edges only)
- A capture-phase `pointerdown` handler in [GraphEditor.tsx](src/modeler/vpl/GraphEditor.tsx) (mirrors the RMB-through-edges handler). LMB-press on a `.react-flow__edge` and hold ~550ms (staying within ~6px) → split the wire and drop a reroute that then follows the cursor until release (`insertRerouteOnEdge`). Early movement / quick release before the threshold cancels, so wire double-click-delete, edge selection, and RMB-pan are untouched. The handler targets `.react-flow__edge` ONLY — it never intercepts reroute nodes, so repositioning stays a normal RF node drag (and thus works inside a multi-selection).
- Secondary path: the connection-drop menu (drag a wire off a port, release on empty canvas) gains a **"Reroute"** entry, shown only when `origin.kind === 'output'` (`addRerouteAndConnect`). `getOriginPortInfo` has a `reroute` branch so dragging from a reroute carries its category/dataType (chains keep their type + highlight).
- **A freshly created reroute is the SOLE selection.** Both creation paths (`insertRerouteOnEdge` for the press-and-hold, `addRerouteAndConnect` for the menu) deselect every other node (and, for the wire-split path, every edge) when appending the new reroute — `makeRerouteNode` marks it `selected: true`. Pressing on a wire to start the hold does NOT otherwise clear a prior node selection, so without this the reposition drag that immediately follows the hold would drag along whatever the user had selected before (the reported "another node was wrongly moved along" bug).
- Delete = plain delete (the reroute + all its links); React Flow's `deleteElements` removes connected edges, and `deleteSelection` does NOT filter reroutes (only macro boundary nodes).

### Compiler layer (collapse pass — zero per-target emit changes)
- [rerouteCollapse.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/rerouteCollapse.ts) — `collapseReroutes(nodes, edges) → { nodes, edges }`. Removes reroute nodes and rewrites each real consumer's edge to the real source it ultimately relays from, resolving chains transitively (memoized + cycle-guarded). Mirrors `injectLinkedOutputMappings` / `canonicalizeAccessorEdges`. **Hot-path no-op** (returns the same arrays) when no reroutes — the overwhelmingly common case.
- Sound in BOTH sync and async modes (a reroute is a pure wire relay — collapsing changes neither read/write ordering nor any value), so unlike accessor-CSE there is **no async gate**.
- Wired into all three compilers BEFORE linked-OM / CSE / `buildAdjacency` so nothing downstream sees a reroute: **JS** at the top of `compileGraph` (+ also collapse each `MacroDef`'s internal graph, since JS inlines macros lazily); **WASM** / **WebGPU** right after `expandMacros` (so in-macro reroutes, now flattened, collapse for free). `A → R → B` compiles byte-identically to `A → B`. Verified: collapse unit (chain / fan-out / dangling / no-op), JS full-compile byte-identical on Game of Life, and WASM + WebGPU worker runs of a reroute model with zero errors.

## Composite Value Types — Vector & Color + Get Self Handle

Two new **bundled value port types** (the Unreal/Blender Make-Break pattern) so a graph passes a whole vector or colour on ONE wire instead of per-component scalars, plus a small agent node exposing the self id.

### Data types ([types.ts](src/modeler/vpl/types.ts) `PortDataType`)
- **`vector`** — a 2D/3D vector value (the Z component exists only in a 3D model; the Z port is hidden via `hiddenPorts` keyed on `is3dModelLike`).
- **`color`** — RGBA (0–255 channels, A defaults 255).
- Both are **EDITOR SUGAR**, not a runtime value type — they're LOWERED to scalar nodes before any per-target compile (see "All-target lowering" below). So the compilers carry **zero** vector/colour-specific emit; vectors run NATIVELY on JS / WASM / WebGPU via the verified scalar emitters (NOT a JS-only clamp — this is the ALL-TARGET DELIVERY rule).

### Nodes (6)
- **Get Self Handle** ([GetSelfHandleNode.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/GetSelfHandleNode.ts), `getSelfHandle`, agent/`bondGraph`): outputs the current agent's own id (`const _v<id> = idx`). Feed it to the by-id nodes (Get/Set Agent Attribute, Get Agent Position/Offset, Form/Break Bond). Emitted on **all three** agent targets (JS / WASM `localGet(idxLocal)` / WebGPU `f32(idx)`) + in `AGENT_WASM_SUPPORTED_TYPES` + `AGENT_WEBGPU_SUPPORTED_TYPES`. (This one is a real node, not lowered.) **In `NEVER_INVARIANT`** ([loopInvariant.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/loopInvariant.ts)) — it has NO value inputs and emits the bare loop variable `idx`, so without it loop-invariance treats it as vacuously invariant and hoists it ABOVE the per-agent `for (let idx…)` decode (JS `idx is not defined`; WASM silently reads `idxLocal`=0 → always handle 0). Surfaces the moment Get Self Handle feeds a by-id node (e.g. Get Agent Position's `Reference` / Form Bond).
- **Make Vector** / **Break Vector** / **Vector Op** (Add/Subtract/Scale/Dot/Cross/Length/Normalize/Distance/Negate/Lerp) — Vector Op + Break Vector are multi-output (in `MULTI_OUTPUT_TYPES` for the editor; the compiler never sees them). Vector Op's `hiddenPorts` shows ports per op (B for binary ops, Scalar for scale, T for lerp, `value` for Dot/Length/Distance else `result`).
- **Make Color** / **Break Color** (`makeColor`/`breakColor`) over `[r, g, b, a]`.

### All-target lowering ([expandComposites.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/expandComposites.ts)) — the key design
A shared pre-compile graph transform rewrites every composite node into plain **`arithmeticOperator` + `getConstant`** scalar nodes BEFORE any target compiles — exactly the pattern of `expandMacros` / `collapseReroutes` / `injectLinkedOutputMappings`. Because the output is ordinary scalar nodes whose JS/WASM/WebGPU emit is already verified by every math-heavy model (Gray-Scott, MNCA, chemistry), vectors run natively on all targets with NO per-target vector code. The SAME deterministic transform runs in ALL 5 compiler front-ends, so the lowered graph is identical across targets and parity is inherited from the scalar primitives.
- **Lowering, by node:** Make Vector/Color → its output's component sources ARE its scalar inputs (pass-through). Break Vector/Color → each scalar output resolves to one component of the input composite. Vector Op → a small tree of `arithmeticOperator` nodes per component (add/sub/scale/cross/normalize/negate/lerp) or one scalar result (dot/length/distance). `lengthOf` = √(Σ component²); normalize divides each by the shared length (the `/` op's ÷0-guard makes a zero vector normalize to zero).
- **Recursive component resolution** (`vecComponentsOf` / `scalarSourceOf` / `resolveScalarInput` / `resolveVecInput`, memoised + cycle-guarded): a vector value bottoms out at a real scalar source or a literal, through any nesting (Vector Op → Vector Op), Break→Make round-trips, and shared values (one Vector Op result → many consumers synthesises once). Literals connect as an inline `_port_*` when the target port has an inline widget, else a synthetic `getConstant`.
- **Wired into all 5 front-ends** after `collapseReroutes`: JS `compileGraph` + `compileAgentGraph`, `compileGraphWasm`, `compileGraphWebGPU`, and BOTH `flattenAgentGraph`s (WASM + WebGPU) — the agent flatten is shared by the gate + the emitter, so a vector agent graph LOWERS to arithmeticOperator/getConstant (which ARE in the agent allowlists) and the gate now ACCEPTS it (runs on WASM/WebGPU, no JS clamp). Hot-path no-op when the graph has no composite nodes (every non-vector model + the 8 agent samples are byte-identical).

### Port wiring + UX
- `isValidConnection` ([GraphEditor.tsx](src/modeler/vpl/GraphEditor.tsx)): a composite port connects ONLY to the SAME composite type — never to a scalar or `any` port (so the lowering always has a well-defined producer). `portsCompatible` handles the suggestion layer (`a === b`).
- Distinct pin colours: vector = teal (`.handleVector`), color = pink (`.handleColor`) in [CaNode.module.css](src/modeler/vpl/CaNode.module.css) + `portHandleClass`. Vector Op op-dropdown UI in CaNode.
- **Apply Force "Vector input" mode** (`config.vectorInput`, default off): an additive `force` vector port that, when on, replaces the X/Y/Z component ports (`hiddenPorts`). `expandComposites` lowers it to the existing fx/fy/fz scalar ports (sets `vectorInput` false + wires the resolved components), so vector mode runs on **all** agent targets — `applyForce.compile` + the WASM/WebGPU emitters are component-only (no per-target vector path).
- Catalogue: **113 selectable** node types (116 − 3 hidden macro), **40 agent** nodes. *(Later raised to **115 selectable** / **42 agent** by the Agent Output-Mapping Graphs + Sprites milestone — `agentOutputMapping` + `setAgentSprite`.)*

### Verified (cross-target, runtime — NOT JS-only)
All 10 Vector Ops compile clean on JS/WASM/WebGPU (grid) + correct JS values (add=4, dot=11, cross=0, length=2.236, normalize=0.447, distance=2.828, negate=−1, lerp=2, scale=2, subtract=−2); **WASM grid runtime** (length model → all cells 5) + **WebGPU grid runtime** (add+break → all cells 4) via the real worker; wired Make Vector input → 5, Break→Make round-trip → 13; the **agent gate now returns `true`** for a reachable vector graph on BOTH WASM + WebGPU (was JS-clamped), Apply Force vector mode emits the lowered `_agentForceX[idx] += _v__vec0`; colour Make/Break works; Game of Life byte-identical on all 3 targets (lowering is a no-op for non-vector graphs); all 8 agent samples (+ synthetic 3D-field) still JS↔WASM bit-parity; tsc + build clean.

---

## Bond-Graph Agents — Floating Cells (milestone, branch `agents_floating_cells`)

A **second, co-resident engine** the sim worker owns alongside the lattice CA:
floating, continuous-position "cells" (AGENTS) that sense each other (NEIGHBOUR
access), are pushed by **graph-authored forces** (flocking/chemotaxis) AND the
engine soft-sphere, join by **bonds**, **grow**, and **divide along their tension
axis** into a connected tissue, with a **closed agent↔grid feedback** (the cell
CA *is* the morphogen field). The largest net-new subsystem GenesisCA has added.
**The agent ENGINE/driver runs on JS** (Phase F ports it to WASM later); the
**GRID/CELL FIELD has an INDEPENDENT compile target** (Decision D-TARGET, fully
relaxed in Phase 2). A WASM grid shares `wasmMemory` views with the JS agent (no
bridge — the agent reads/writes `readAttrs` directly). A **WebGPU grid** now works
too (e.g. chemical diffusion on the GPU) via a per-step **field-readback bridge**:
when the agent graph uses the field, the worker reads the GPU attrs back to CPU
before the agent gather and uploads the deposit before the cell step — so the grid
gets the GPU fast path while agents stay JS. The independent agent target lives on
`CenterBasedConfig.agentTarget` (`'js'` default; WASM/WebGPU agent loops are
Phase F — `agentTargetOf` clamps to `'js'` until they land). The JS/WASM step loop
is byte-identical (the agent interleave is ONLY in the WebGPU step branch). The
agent COMPUTE is 3D-aware (the engine runAgentStep / divideAgent eigensolve + the
JS *and* WASM neighbour-query emitters all handle a `W×H×D` world), and gl3d.ts
now ALSO renders 3D agents (sphere impostors + bond lines — see the control-
overhaul section; sprites stay 2D-billboard-only).
Three shipped samples
exercise the breadth: **Boids — Flocking** (neighbour access + Apply Force +
momentum), **Morphogenesis — Differential Tissue** (asymmetric division +
maturity + contact inhibition = specialization, not a uniform blob), **Chemotaxis
— Aggregation** (the field bridge + WASM-decoupled diffusion). Runbook:
[docs/HANDOFF_AGENTS_FLOATING_CELLS.md](docs/HANDOFF_AGENTS_FLOATING_CELLS.md).

### Topology + the two-graph split
- `CAModel.topologyMode { gridCells, agents }` (the 3D milestone shipped the schema; this milestone un-gated `agents`). A model may have BOTH (agents additive on top of the always-present grid — there is NO lattice-less branch in v1, "grid required").
- `CAModel.agentGraphNodes?`/`agentGraphEdges?` — a SECOND rule graph (the off-lattice agent behaviour). `graphNodes`/`graphEdges` is unchanged (the Cells graph, zero migration); `macroDefs` is SHARED. `SET_AGENT_GRAPH`/`setAgentGraph` reducer + callback (wired into BOTH useMemo dep arrays). `stringifyCompact` emits them one-line-per-item.
- **The editor swaps which graph it shows behind ONE ReactFlow instance** ([GraphEditor.tsx](src/modeler/vpl/GraphEditor.tsx)): `activeGraph: 'cells'|'agents'` (persisted in [modelerUiState.ts](src/modeler/modelerUiState.ts); mirrored to the `activeGraphKind` module global in [graphState.ts](src/modeler/vpl/graphState.ts)). The scope-switch effect forks its root branch + the initial seed on `activeGraph` (`activeGraph` in the dep array → `clearHistory()` on swap = cross-graph-undo guard for free). `scheduleSync` routes a root edit to SET_GRAPH vs SET_AGENT_GRAPH; a synchronous `flushSync` runs before every sub-tab swap (the debounce-vs-swap race). A Cells/Agents pill strip (top-centre, sibling of the macro breadcrumb), shown only when Agents is on; disabling Agents auto-switches to Cells. Clipboard is graph-kind-tagged (cross-graph paste rejected). The 6 ModelContext cleanup cascades (`patchAllNodes`) now scan `agentGraphNodes` too (the `_undef`-strand fix).
- **Palette / quick-add / connection-drop gate by the active sub-tab** ([nodeValidation.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/nodeValidation.ts) `isNodeAvailable`, reading `getActiveGraphKind()`): agent nodes (`requirements.bondGraph`) only on Agents; `LATTICE_ONLY_TYPES` (cell event roots, the neighbour/NI family, neighbourhood writes, Get Cell Position) hidden on Agents; **universal** nodes (arithmetic, conditionals, Get/Set Attribute, Get Random, Set Cell Looks, …) available in BOTH. Palette + Attributes panel re-render on swap via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeActiveGraphKind)`.

### Generic Agent Platform — separate agent attributes / variables + the comprehensive node catalogue (current)
Agents are now first-class authorable entities with their OWN state, NOT a reuse of the cell attribute set. **This SUPERSEDES the old "cell attrs DOUBLE as agent attrs" framing.**
- **Agent attributes** — a parallel `CAModel.agentAttributes?: Attribute[]` (agent-only per-agent state, a SEPARATE id-space). The agent SoA + the own-agent channel `r_<id>`/`w_<id>` (behaviour/division loops) are keyed by these via **`agentAttrsOf`** ([src/model/attributeScope.ts](src/model/attributeScope.ts), the single source of truth: `agentAttrsOf` / `cellAttrsOf` / `cellFieldAttrsOf` / `cellFieldWriteAttrsOf`). On the Agents tab the Attributes panel lists `agentAttributes` with its own +Add (ADD/UPDATE/REMOVE_AGENT_ATTRIBUTE reducers); Get/Set/Update Attribute + Get/Set Agent Attribute resolve their dropdown against `agentAttrsOf` (graph-aware via `getActiveGraphKind()`). **Master-detail wiring (bug-fixed):** `AttributesPanelContent` already auto-selects a new agent attr on +Add and resolves the `mode='detail'` editor against `agentAttributes`, but `ModelerView.selectedItemName` (which GATES the second detail PanelShell's visibility via `detailItemName != null`) resolved `attr:<id>` ONLY against `model.attributes` — so an agent-attribute selection returned null and the editor never mounted (creating OR clicking an agent attr did nothing). Fixed by making `selectedItemName` graph-kind-aware: `ModelerView` subscribes to `getActiveGraphKind` and, when the Agents tab is active (`attrAgentMode`), resolves `attr:` against `agentAttributes` THEN the shared MODEL attributes (`isModelAttribute` — a cell attr, not listed on the Agents tab, must NOT resolve there) and `var:` against `agentVariables`. Verified in-app: selecting `alive` and +Add both open the detail editor on the Agents tab.
- **Cell-attribute agent-access permission** — `Attribute.agentAccess?: 'none'|'read'|'readWrite'` on CELL attributes gates the field bridge: `_field_<id>` is threaded ONLY for `cellFieldAttrsOf` (agentAccess !== 'none'); field READ nodes (sampleField/fieldGradient/readCellsUnder) require read, field WRITE nodes (affectCellsUnder/secreteToField) require readWrite. CA cells can NEVER read agent attributes (structural — the cell compilers never see `agentAttributes`). Edited via the "Agent access" control in the cell-attribute detail editor.
- **Agent variables** — a parallel `CAModel.agentVariables?: Variable[]`; `buildVariableJS(variables)` takes a list (cell sites pass `model.variables`, agent sites pass `model.agentVariables`); the variable reducer actions carry `target: 'cell'|'agent'`. The agent-WASM array-var gate reads `agentVariables` (the false-positive fix). The JS compiler's **`sourceYieldsArray`** (which decides array-pass-through vs `[_v]`-wrap when a value feeds an `isArray` input like Get Array Element / Array Length / Aggregate) must ALSO look up BOTH scopes — without the `agentVariables` arm an agent Local-Variable ARRAY was mis-classified as a scalar and wrapped as `[_v]`, so `arrayElement[0]` returned the WHOLE array and `arrayElement[≥1]` returned 0 (`[_v].length===1`), silently corrupting read-modify-write accumulators (e.g. a per-agent force-accumulation loop). Fixed to search `variables` then `agentVariables` (ids are globally unique → unambiguous).
- **Migrations** ([agentAttributeSplitMigration.ts](src/model/agentAttributeSplitMigration.ts) + [variableScopeMigration.ts](src/model/variableScopeMigration.ts), LOAD_MODEL): legacy agent models stored agent state in cell attributes/variables. The split migrations MOVE any cell attr/var referenced as agent state by the agent graph into `agentAttributes`/`agentVariables` (DUPLICATE if also cell-referenced); a cell attr used as a FIELD stays a cell attr + gets `agentAccess`. Idempotent (skip when the agent set is non-empty). Verified: Tissue→agentAttributes=[maturity]; Chemotaxis→chemical stays a cell field (readWrite); Boids→accumulators move to agentVariables; lattice byte-identity preserved.
- **ABI-mirror discipline** — `buildAgentLoopParams`↔`buildAgentLoopArgs` and `buildDivisionParams`↔`buildDivisionArgs` both derive `r_`/`w_` from `agentAttrsOf` and `_field_` from `cellFieldAttrsOf`/the worker's `fieldSpecs` (a separate ordered list from the agent `attrSpecs`); `_lookupTables` rides a pinned slot (after glyphColors, before the field block) gated on the model having a lookupTable model attr. The worker's `buildAgentAttrSpecs` maps `agentAttrs` (from the init message's `agentAttributes`), and `computeAgentMemoryLayout` + the agent-WASM spec derive from the same `agentAttrsOf` list (the baked-offset lockstep).
- **Universal-node agent-compile fixes** (`compileAgentGraph` ran none of the cell pre-resolves): now runs the indicator-index pre-resolve, the Stop Event `_stopIdx`/`stopMessages` collection (offset by the cell stop count — shared `_stopFlag`), and the Set Cell Looks `_isV_` viewer-hoist into the behaviour + division preambles, over `agentGraphNodes`. Accessor-CSE is gated OFF in async agent mode (`agentUpdateMode` default 'async').
- **Comprehensive agent node catalogue** (new `bondGraph` nodes, JS-only — clones of the lattice array ops with the NeighborIndex codec removed: integer agent ids, `-1` empty sentinel, reading the agent SoA at `r_<attr>[id]`): **Get Bonded Agents** (this agent's bonded partners → id[], the data sibling of For Each Bond), **Filter Agents** / **Join Agents** (multi-output result+count over id arrays), **Pick Random Agent** / **Pick N Random Agents** (shared `_rs`), **Get Agents Attribute** (the keystone gather: one attr over an id array → values[], for Aggregate/Group Counting), **Set Agents Attribute** (write-many), **Set Velocity** (writes `_agentVX/VY[idx]`; needs momentum>0). Registered like the lattice array producers (scratch `_v<id>_result`/`_vals`/`_work`, `varName`, MULTI_OUTPUT for filter/join, NEVER_INVARIANT + NEVER_PURE). Excluded from `AGENT_WASM_SUPPORTED_TYPES` so a graph using one clamps to JS. Plan: [docs/PLAN_GENERIC_AGENT_PLATFORM.md](docs/PLAN_GENERIC_AGENT_PLATFORM.md).
- **Graph-authored agent spawning + the Agent Init Event (DONE — PR4):** a once-only **`agentInit`** entry-point (runs ONCE on first load AND on Reset, before the first behaviour step — the agent analogue of the cell Init Event) whose `DO` chain the user loops over to spawn agents. **`createAgent`** (multi-output: `x`/`y`[/`z` in 3D]/`radius` inputs → a `handle` id; compiles to `const _v<id>_handle = _agentCreate(x, y[, z], radius)` — the `type` input was **REMOVED**) stages a slot at `alive=0`; **`addAgentToWorld`** (`handle` input) commits it (`alive=1`, `liveCount++`); a leak-sweep frees any Created-but-not-Added slot at the end of init. **`setAgentPosition`/`setAgentRadius`** + the existing `setAgentAttribute` retarget a staged handle (the `setAgentType` node was **DELETED** with the built-in `type`) (the setters relax their range guard to `< _agentMaxAgents` when `ctx.agentRoot==='init'`, since the staged slot isn't yet `alive`). `buildAgentInitParams`↔`buildAgentInitArgs` mirror (leads with `_agentCreate,_agentAddToWorld,_agentMaxAgents`). `agentInit`+`createAgent` are in `MULTI_OUTPUT_TYPES`. The worker's `runAgentInit` closures `_agentCreate`=allocAgentSlot+initAgentSlot(stage alive=0) / `_agentAddToWorld`=alive=1; overflow (maxAgents) surfaces an `agentOverflow` notice (never wraps). **Two engine fixes the samples surfaced:** (1) the **cell Init Event now runs on first load** too (worker `init` handler calls `runInit()` after `initGrid` applied defaults — BEFORE the agent Init Event so a Create-Agent rule that reads the field sees the seeded substrate), so a procedural-init model shows its state on load instead of a blank grid until Reset; an embedded `simulationState` still wins (pendingSimStateRestore overwrites after the first step — verified on Amphiphile). (2) **sync agent mode** (`agentUpdateMode:'sync'`) double-buffers the agent attrs, so `runAgentInit` copies `attrWrite→attrRead` after the init (the init's Set-Agent-Attribute wrote the write buffer; the first step + getState read the read buffer) — without it a sync model loads with all-default agent state.
- **RNG-in-loop hoist fix (sinkAnalysis):** `hoistPastLoops` would hoist an RNG node (`getRandom` / `pickRandom*` / `groupOperator|aggregate` random/weightedRandom) OUT of a Loop/ForEach body, so a spawn loop that randomises each agent gave every agent the SAME value (computed once). `isLoopPinned(nodeId)` (a backward closure: self-or-any-value-input is RNG) keeps such values per-iteration. Lattice byte-identical (only relocates RNG emission, and only when an RNG value sits inside a loop body). Verified: 64-agent spawn grid → per-agent random state 31/64; standard lattice models compile clean.
- **Two shipped samples (PR5):** **Game of Life on Agents** ([scripts/gen-gol-agents.mjs](scripts/gen-gol-agents.mjs)) — the GENERICITY PROOF: Conway's GoL on a 32×32 grid of agents spawned by the Agent Init Event, the totalistic rule via Get Nearby Agents → Get Agents Attribute → Aggregate(sum) → Compare/Logic, SYNC agent update, `customForcesOnly`+momentum 0 (pinned grid). Verified: alive count evolves (318→…→stabilising still-lifes/blinkers), renders as green/dark circles. **Ant Necrophoresis** ([scripts/gen-ant-necrophoresis.mjs](scripts/gen-ant-necrophoresis.mjs)) — STIGMERGY via the closed field bridge: a `corpse` cell field (agentAccess readWrite) seeded ~18% by the cell Init Event; ants (`carrying` bool agent attr) random-walk (Apply Force), sense local density (Read Cells Under), and pick up isolated corpses / drop on clusters via inverse sigmoids (Affect Cells Under) — autocatalytic piles. Verified: piles form (max 1→5, 0→18 cells with ≥3). **Deposit-radius gotcha:** `affectCellsUnder` with radius 0 writes NOTHING (agents sit at fractional positions, so the disk membership `dx²+dy²≤0` matches no integer-indexed cell) — use radius ≥ ~0.71 to reliably hit the cell(s) under a moving agent.
